Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the nucleus structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 432. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 303 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to nucleus structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 432/303 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the nucleus space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 493 and the convergence rate is O(288/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 493 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the nucleus structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 288. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
